   1++6F9B              ; ============================================================================
   2++6F9B              ; Project127 ATM Core — linked kernel image layout measurement
   3++6F9B              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++6F9B              ;
   5++6F9B              ; Measures the actual assembled Stage2 kernel span [p127_entry,p127_end).
   6++6F9B              ; It is not a fixed reservation: the linked end label determines the size.
   7++6F9B              ; The current loader contract places the image at 6000h and bounds it by A000h.
   8++6F9B              ; Both live host pages used by that CPU window (P5 and P2) must be RESERVED.
   9++6F9B              ; This module validates placement only; it does not relocate or map pages.
  10++6F9B              ; ============================================================================
  11++6F9B              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  12++6F9B              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  13++6F9B              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  14++6F9B              P127_LAYOUT_START         EQU 06000h
  15++6F9B              P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT         EQU 0A000h
  16++6F9B              P127_LAYOUT_MAX_BYTES     EQU P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT-P127_LAYOUT_START
  17++6F9B
  18++6F9B              p127_layout_selftest:
  19++6F9B                      ; The linked entry must match the BASIC loader's CODE address.
  20++6F9B 21 00 60             ld      hl,p127_entry
  21++6F9E 11 00 60             ld      de,P127_LAYOUT_START
  22++6FA1 B7                   or      a
  23++6FA2 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  24++6FA4 C2 E3 6F             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  25++6FA7
  26++6FA7                      ; Require a non-empty image and an end no higher than the UI window.
  27++6FA7 21 E5 95             ld      hl,p127_end
  28++6FAA 11 00 60             ld      de,p127_entry
  29++6FAD B7                   or      a
  30++6FAE ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  31++6FB0 DA E3 6F             jp      c,p127_layout_reject
  32++6FB3 CA E3 6F             jp      z,p127_layout_reject
  33++6FB6 22 FD 6F             ld      (p127_kernel_size),hl
  34++6FB9 7C                   ld      a,h
  35++6FBA FE 41                cp      041h
  36++6FBC D2 E3 6F             jp      nc,p127_layout_reject
  37++6FBF
  38++6FBF 21 E5 95             ld      hl,p127_end
  39++6FC2 11 00 A0             ld      de,P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT
  40++6FC5 B7                   or      a
  41++6FC6 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  42++6FC8 DA D1 6F             jp      c,p127_layout_end_ok
  43++6FCB CA D1 6F             jp      z,p127_layout_end_ok
  44++6FCE C3 E3 6F             jp      p127_layout_reject
  45++6FD1              p127_layout_end_ok:
  46++6FD1                      ; P5 backs the #6000..#7FFF portion; P2 backs #8000..#9FFF.
  47++6FD1 3A B1 75             ld      a,(p127_mem_page_state+5)
  48++6FD4 FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
  49++6FD6 C2 E3 6F             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  50++6FD9 3A AE 75             ld      a,(p127_mem_page_state+2)
  51++6FDC FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
  52++6FDE C2 E3 6F             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  53++6FE1 B7                   or      a
  54++6FE2 C9                   ret
  55++6FE3
  56++6FE3              p127_layout_reject:
  57++6FE3 37                   scf
  58++6FE4 C9                   ret
  59++6FE5
  60++6FE5              ; Report the measured byte count in hexadecimal, high byte first.
  61++6FE5              p127_layout_report:

  63++6FE8 CD 21 63             call    p127_puts
  64++6FEB 3A FE 6F             ld      a,(p127_kernel_size+1)

 475+ 7037                      INCLUDE "src/kernel/p127_resident.asm"
# file opened: p127_resident.asm
   1++7037              ; ============================================================================
   2++7037              ; Project127 — resident-window contract / software preflight
   3++7037              ; Module version: 0.4.0
   4++7037              ; Defines the only first-stage remap candidate without touching hardware.
   5++7037              ; W1/P5 owns resident execution+data; W2/P2 owns resident stack/control.
   6++7037              ; W3 is the only candidate target and may be used only after UI quiescence.
   7++7037              ; W0 is ROM. No OUT instruction is permitted here.
   8++7037              ; ============================================================================
   9++7037              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  10++7037              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  11++7037              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  12++7037
  13++7037              ; The remap-critical resident module is deliberately placed first after the
  14++7037              ; Stage2 boot body. W1/P5 is the fixed execution/data window; W2/P2 is the
  15++7037              ; fixed stack window; W3 is the only target. This preflight checks the linked
  16++7037              ; addresses and current live stack at runtime. It performs no page OUT.
  17++7037              p127_resident_start:
  18++7037
  19++7037              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW  EQU 1
  20++7037              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_PAGE    EQU 5
  21++7037              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW EQU 2
  22++7037              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_PAGE   EQU 2
  23++7037              P127_RESIDENT_TARGET       EQU 3
  24++7037              P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED  EQU 1
  25++7037
  26++7037              ; IN: A=target window, B=resident code window, C=resident stack window,
  27++7037              ;     D=flags (bit 0 set means UI is quiesced).
  28++7037              ; OUT: carry=0 if the fixed resident contract is satisfied; carry=1 otherwise.
  29++7037              ; This is a software contract check, not a physical mapping operation.
  30++7037              p127_resident_validate:
  31++7037 32 C1 70             ld      (p127_resident_arg_target),a
  32++703A 78                   ld      a,b
  33++703B 32 C2 70             ld      (p127_resident_arg_code),a
  34++703E 79                   ld      a,c
  35++703F 32 C3 70             ld      (p127_resident_arg_stack),a
  36++7042 7A                   ld      a,d
  37++7043 32 C4 70             ld      (p127_resident_arg_flags),a
  38++7046
  39++7046 3A C1 70             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)
  40++7049 FE 03                cp      P127_RESIDENT_TARGET
  41++704B C2 75 70             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  42++704E 3A C2 70             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_code)
  43++7051 FE 01                cp      P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW
  44++7053 C2 75 70             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  45++7056 3A C3 70             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_stack)
  46++7059 FE 02                cp      P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW
  47++705B C2 75 70             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  48++705E 3A C4 70             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_flags)
  49++7061 E6 01                and     P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED
  50++7063 CA 75 70             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  51++7066                      ; Explicitly assert target is distinct from both resident windows.
  52++7066 3A C1 70             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)
  53++7069 FE 01                cp      P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW
  54++706B CA 75 70             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  55++706E FE 02                cp      P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW
  56++7070 CA 75 70             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  57++7073 B7                   or      a
  58++7074 C9                   ret
  59++7075
  60++7075              p127_resident_reject:
  61++7075 37                   scf
  62++7076 C9                   ret
  63++7077
  64++7077              ; Synthetic software-only tests: accepted W3 with UI quiesced; reject W1,
  65++7077              ; reject W2, reject active UI, and reject a displaced resident code window.
  66++7077              p127_resident_selftest:
  67++7077 3E 03                ld      a,3
  68++7079 06 01                ld      b,1
  69++707B 0E 02                ld      c,2
  70++707D 16 01                ld      d,1
  71++707F CD 37 70             call    p127_resident_validate
  72++7082 DA BF 70             jp      c,p127_resident_test_fail
  73++7085
  74++7085 3E 01                ld      a,1
  75++7087 06 01                ld      b,1
  76++7089 0E 02                ld      c,2
  77++708B 16 01                ld      d,1
  78++708D CD 37 70             call    p127_resident_validate
  79++7090 D2 BF 70             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  80++7093
  81++7093 3E 02                ld      a,2
  82++7095 06 01                ld      b,1
  83++7097 0E 02                ld      c,2
  84++7099 16 01                ld      d,1
  85++709B CD 37 70             call    p127_resident_validate
  86++709E D2 BF 70             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  87++70A1
  88++70A1 3E 03                ld      a,3
  89++70A3 06 01                ld      b,1
  90++70A5 0E 02                ld      c,2
  91++70A7 16 00                ld      d,0
  92++70A9 CD 37 70             call    p127_resident_validate
  93++70AC D2 BF 70             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  94++70AF
  95++70AF 3E 03                ld      a,3
  96++70B1 06 00                ld      b,0
  97++70B3 0E 02                ld      c,2
  98++70B5 16 01                ld      d,1
  99++70B7 CD 37 70             call    p127_resident_validate
 100++70BA D2 BF 70             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
 101++70BD B7                   or      a
 102++70BE C9                   ret
 103++70BF
 104++70BF              p127_resident_test_fail:
 105++70BF 37                   scf
 106++70C0 C9                   ret
 107++70C1
 108++70C1              p127_resident_arg_target:
 109++70C1 00                   db      0
 110++70C2              p127_resident_arg_code:
 111++70C2 00                   db      0
 112++70C3              p127_resident_arg_stack:
 113++70C3 00                   db      0
 114++70C4              p127_resident_arg_flags:
 115++70C4 00                   db      0
 116++70C5
 117++70C5
 118++70C5              ; W3 ownership / UI quiesce state machine. Software only; no page OUT.
 119++70C5              P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE EQU 1
 120++70C5              P127_W3_STATE_QUIESCED EQU 2
 121++70C5              P127_W3_OWNER_UI EQU 1
 122++70C5              P127_W3_OWNER_NONE EQU 0
 123++70C5              P127_W3_OWNER_HANDOFF EQU 2
 124++70C5
 125++70C5              p127_w3_init:
 126++70C5 3E 01                ld      a,P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 127++70C7 32 62 72             ld      (p127_w3_state),a
 128++70CA 3E 01                ld      a,P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 129++70CC 32 63 72             ld      (p127_w3_owner),a
 130++70CF AF                   xor     a
 131++70D0 32 64 72             ld      (p127_w3_busy),a
 132++70D3 B7                   or      a
 133++70D4 C9                   ret
 134++70D5
 135++70D5              p127_w3_ui_enter:
 136++70D5 3A 62 72             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 137++70D8 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 138++70DA 20 79                jr      nz,p127_w3_reject
 139++70DC 3A 63 72             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 140++70DF FE 01                cp      P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 141++70E1 20 72                jr      nz,p127_w3_reject
 142++70E3 3A 64 72             ld      a,(p127_w3_busy)
 143++70E6 B7                   or      a
 144++70E7 20 6C                jr      nz,p127_w3_reject
 145++70E9 3E 01                ld      a,1
 146++70EB 32 64 72             ld      (p127_w3_busy),a
 147++70EE B7                   or      a
 148++70EF C9                   ret
 149++70F0
 150++70F0              p127_w3_ui_leave:
 151++70F0 AF                   xor     a
 152++70F1 32 64 72             ld      (p127_w3_busy),a
 153++70F4 C9                   ret
 154++70F5
 155++70F5              p127_w3_quiesce:

 348++7268              ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 349++7268              ; Stage 2.10 — real-address resident trampoline preflight (software only).
 350++7268              ; Requires DI on entry; DI is asserted again and interrupts remain disabled.
 351++7268              ; Z80 does not provide a general instruction to read/restore prior IFF1, so
 352++7268              ; this entry contract intentionally does not attempt an EI/IFF round trip.
 353++7268              ; W1 is #4000-#7FFF; W2 is #8000-#BFFF. The linked resident module must fit
 354++7268              ; wholly in W1, all resident state is inside that module, and the live stack
 355++7268              ; must be in W2. No page register is written.
 356++7268              p127_resident_trampoline_preflight:

 358++7269                      ; Linked code and data must remain wholly inside the fixed W1 window.
 359++7269 21 37 70             ld      hl,p127_resident_start
 360++726C 11 00 60             ld      de,06000h
 361++726F B7                   or      a
 362++7270 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 363++7272 DA B5 72             jp      c,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 364++7275
 365++7275 21 B7 72             ld      hl,p127_resident_end
 366++7278 11 00 80             ld      de,08000h
 367++727B B7                   or      a
 368++727C ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 369++727E D2 B5 72             jp      nc,p127_resident_trampoline_fail

 380++7293 B7                   or      a
 381++7294 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 382++7296 D2 B5 72             jp      nc,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 383++7299

 385++7299                      ; still be within W2, independent of W3's mapping.
 386++7299 21 00 00             ld      hl,0
 387++729C 39                   add     hl,sp
 388++729D 11 00 B0             ld      de,0B000h

   1++72B7              ; ============================================================================
   2++72B7              ; Project127 ATM Core — physical page-state manager
   3++72B7              ; Module version: 0.4.4
   4++72B7              ; ABI: reserve A=first page, B=count; alloc -> A=page, C=exhausted;
   5++72B7              ;      free A=page, C=invalid/not allocated.
   6++72B7              ; Model: 64 physical 16 KiB page IDs = exactly 1 MiB.
   7++72B7              ; ATM Turbo 2+ page IDs are physical 16 KiB RAM pages. Bootstrap reservations
   8++72B7              ; reflect the active ZX-128 map: page 5 (window 1), page 2 (window 2), and
   9++72B7              ; page 0 (window 3). Page 5 is already covered by the video-page union.
  10++72B7              ; ============================================================================

   1++762C              ; ============================================================================
   2++762C              ; Project127 ATM Core — UINT32 physical range validator
   3++762C              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++762C              ; ABI:
   5++762C              ;   p127_range_start  = 4-byte little-endian physical start address
   6++762C              ;   p127_range_length = 4-byte little-endian byte length
   7++762C              ;   call p127_range_validate; carry=1 if invalid, clear if valid; clobbers AF, B, DE, HL
   8++762C              ; Semantics: half-open [start, start+length), bounded by 1 MiB.
   9++762C              ; Zero-length ranges are valid when start <= 1 MiB.
  10++762C              ; This is arithmetic/accounting only; it does not map or access RAM.
  11++762C              ; ============================================================================

  13++762C              P127_RANGE_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  14++762C              P127_RANGE_VERSION_PATCH EQU 2

   1++7746              ; ============================================================================
   2++7746              ; Project127 ATM Core — top-down physical program-page arena
   3++7746              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++7746              ;
   5++7746              ; Arena discovers the highest contiguous FREE physical run from the page-state
   6++7746              ; map. The current reservation map yields P8..P63; P6 remains isolated below
   7++7746              ; reserved P7. Physical pages are 16 KiB and P63 ends at 0x100000.
   8++7746              ;
   9++7746              ; API:
  10++7746              ;   p127_arena_init: discovers and validates the highest contiguous FREE run.
  11++7746              ;   p127_arena_alloc: IN B=page count; OUT A=first page, carry=1 on failure.
  12++7746              ;                    Allocates downward from the current high boundary.
  13++7746              ;   p127_arena_free: IN A=first page, B=count; only most-recent block accepted.
  14++7746              ;                   Frees it and contracts the high boundary upward.
  15++7746              ;
  16++7746              ; This is page-granular accounting only. It does not map pages, access RAM,
  17++7746              ; provide byte-granular allocation, or promise a seamless legacy 16-bit ABI.
  18++7746              ; Free is intentionally LIFO until per-process ownership/coalescing exists.
  19++7746              ; ============================================================================
  20++7746              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  21++7746              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  22++7746              P127_ARENA_VERSION_PATCH EQU 2
  23++7746              P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE      EQU 64
  24++7746              P127_ARENA_PAGE_SIZE      EQU 04000h
  25++7746

  27++7746              ; The first RESERVED/ALLOCATED page below that run is the lower guard.
  28++7746              ; An all-FREE map is rejected: required system reservations are missing.
  29++7746              p127_arena_init:

   1++78A4              ; ============================================================================
   2++78A4              ; Project127 ATM Core — managed logical address-space page table
   3++78A4              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++78A4              ;
   5++78A4              ; 4 MiB logical space: 256 logical pages x 16 KiB. A logical page maps to one
   6++78A4              ; allocator-owned physical ATM page (0..63). Translation metadata only:
   7++78A4              ; no CPU-window mapping, physical byte access, IN/OUT or DMA.
   8++78A4              ;
   9++78A4              ; API:
  10++78A4              ;   p127_as_init: clear all slots to FFh (unmapped).
  11++78A4              ;   p127_as_bind: A=logical page 0..255, B=allocated physical page 0..63.
  12++78A4              ;   p127_as_unbind: A=logical page; removes metadata, does not free RAM.
  13++78A4              ;   p127_as_translate: p127_as_address (uint32 LE) -> A=physical page,
  14++78A4              ;                      HL=14-bit page offset; carry=1 if invalid/unmapped.
  15++78A4              ; Caller must unbind before releasing a physical page.
  16++78A4              ; ============================================================================
  17++78A4              P127_AS_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  18++78A4              P127_AS_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  19++78A4              P127_AS_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0

  21++78A4              P127_AS_PAGE_SIZE EQU 04000h

   5++7B45              ; Metadata only: no byte access/copy, mapping, OUT, DMA, IRQ, IM or EI.

   1++7D25              ; ============================================================================
   2++7D25              ; Project127 — managed logical-span copy
   3++7D25              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++7D25              ;
   5++7D25              ; Copies between non-overlapping mapped logical spans using the guarded W3
   6++7D25              ; lease and a resident 64-byte bounce buffer. W1/P5 holds this code/buffer;
   7++7D25              ; W2/P2 holds the live stack. No UI call occurs while W3 is leased.
   8++7D25              ;
   9++7D25              ; API inputs:
  10++7D25              ;   p127_as_copy_source      uint32 little-endian
  11++7D25              ;   p127_as_copy_destination uint32 little-endian
  12++7D25              ;   p127_as_copy_length      uint32 little-endian
  13++7D25              ; Outputs:
  14++7D25              ;   carry clear = success; carry set = failure
  15++7D25              ;   p127_as_copy_completed   exact bytes written before return
  16++7D25              ;   p127_as_copy_error       0 success, 1 span, 2 overlap, 3 lease,
  17++7D25              ;                            4 source map, 5 destination map, 6 lease restore
  18++7D25              ; Overlap is rejected; this is not memmove. Unexpected runtime failures after
  19++7D25              ; writes begin may leave a destination prefix modified.
  20++7D25              ; ============================================================================
  21++7D25              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  22++7D25              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  23++7D25              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  24++7D25              P127_AS_COPY_BUFFER_SIZE EQU 64

 311++7F4B              ; IN HL=left uint32 pointer, DE=right uint32 pointer.
 312++7F4B              ; OUT carry if left<right; Z if equal. Preserves BC.
 313++7F4B              p127_as_copy_compare_u32:

 178++83D3              ; Safe-remap preparation gate (software-only).
 179++83D3              ; IN: A=target window 0..3, B=new physical page 0..63,
 180++83D3              ;     C=bitmask of protected windows (bit 0=W0 ... bit 3=W3).
 181++83D3              ; OUT: carry=1 if invalid/protected; carry=0 if a pending request is staged.
 182++83D3              ; This routine NEVER writes a page port and NEVER changes the active map.
 183++83D3              ; Caller must include execution, stack, UI, interrupt and host/staging windows
 184++83D3              ; in C. The bootstrap currently protects all four windows (#0F).

  22++85C9              ; Requires interrupts disabled. DI is asserted defensively and this routine
  23++85C9              ; returns with interrupts disabled. IM and interrupt vectors are untouched.
  24++85C9              ; The active stack must be at/above #B900 after this routine's save frame.

   1++8664              ; ============================================================================
   2++8664              ; Project127 TR-DOS directory reader
   3++8664              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++8664              ; Reads track 0 sectors 1..8 via the documented TR-DOS ROM entry #3D13.
   5++8664              ; Directory snapshot: 128 * 16 bytes at #C800 (reserved host/staging page P0).
   6++8664              ; Read-only: no file-data reads, writes, DMA, IM/EI or raw paging OUT.

   9++8664              P127_TRDOS_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1

  12++8664              ; Separate from the live caller stack at #BFFF and handoff probe stack #B800.
  13++8664              ; The 240-byte usable interval #B700..#B7EF is below the handoff canary.
  14++8664              P127_TRDOS_SCRATCH_STACK_TOP EQU 0B7F0h
  15++8664              P127_TRDOS_DIRECTORY_BYTES EQU 0800h

  21++8664              ; Called immediately after the Project126-compatible legacy mapping
  22++8664              ; setup, before p127_ui_init changes the ROM/video/shadow-port state.

   1++8BB1              ; ============================================================================
   2++8BB1              ; Project127 ATM Turbo 2+ beeper output driver
   3++8BB1              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++8BB1              ;
   5++8BB1              ; Hardware: Spectrum/ATM #FE, D4 = speaker, D3 = tape output,
   6++8BB1              ; D0..D2 = border colour. OUT (#FE),A also presents A3 from A's bit 3
   7++8BB1              ; on ATM, so the caller-supplied D3 state is preserved.
   8++8BB1              ;
   9++8BB1              ; This is a low-level output latch only. It does not invent a timer,
  10++8BB1              ; enable interrupts, block the main loop, or emulate an IBM PC speaker.
  11++8BB1              ; The Z80 host owns this physical port. NeoGS must request sound through
  12++8BB1              ; the host transport/DMA and must never issue physical port I/O.
  13++8BB1              ;
  14++8BB1              ; API:
  15++8BB1              ;   p127_speaker_init  IN A = initial #FE low nibble (border + tape)
  16++8BB1              ;   p127_speaker_set   IN A bit 0 = speaker level (0/1)
  17++8BB1              ;   p127_speaker_toggle
  18++8BB1              ;   p127_speaker_stop
  19++8BB1              ;   p127_speaker_get   OUT A = shadow latch
  20++8BB1              ;
  21++8BB1              ; Caller must initialize with the actual current border/tape state before
  22++8BB1              ; using set/toggle/stop. No safe hardware readback of the #FE output latch
  23++8BB1              ; is assumed.
  24++8BB1              ; ============================================================================
  25++8BB1              P127_SPEAKER_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  26++8BB1              P127_SPEAKER_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  27++8BB1              P127_SPEAKER_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  28++8BB1
  29++8BB1              P127_SPEAKER_PORT EQU 0FEh
  30++8BB1              P127_SPEAKER_MASK EQU 010h
  31++8BB1              P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK EQU 00Fh
  32++8BB1
  33++8BB1              p127_speaker_init:
  34++8BB1 E6 0F                and     P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK
  35++8BB3 32 0F 8C             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  36++8BB6 C9                   ret
  37++8BB7
  38++8BB7              ; Set D4 while preserving border D0..D2 and tape D3.
  39++8BB7              ; OUT (#FE),A intentionally uses A3 as part of the ATM border/BRIGHT
  40++8BB7              ; address decode; preserving D3 also preserves that address-line state.
  41++8BB7              p127_speaker_set:
  42++8BB7 C5                   push    bc
  43++8BB8 47                   ld      b,a
  44++8BB9 3A 0F 8C             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  45++8BBC E6 0F                and     P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK
  46++8BBE 4F                   ld      c,a
  47++8BBF 78                   ld      a,b
  48++8BC0 E6 01                and     1
  49++8BC2 28 05                jr      z,p127_speaker_set_low
  50++8BC4 79                   ld      a,c
  51++8BC5 F6 10                or      P127_SPEAKER_MASK
  52++8BC7 18 01                jr      p127_speaker_write
  53++8BC9              p127_speaker_set_low:
  54++8BC9 79                   ld      a,c
  55++8BCA              p127_speaker_write:
  56++8BCA 32 0F 8C             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  57++8BCD D3 FE                out     (P127_SPEAKER_PORT),a
  58++8BCF C1                   pop     bc
  59++8BD0 C9                   ret
  60++8BD1
  61++8BD1              ; Invert only D4. Border/tape state remains unchanged.
  62++8BD1              p127_speaker_toggle:
  63++8BD1 3A 0F 8C             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  64++8BD4 EE 10                xor     P127_SPEAKER_MASK
  65++8BD6 32 0F 8C             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  66++8BD9 D3 FE                out     (P127_SPEAKER_PORT),a
  67++8BDB C9                   ret
  68++8BDC
  69++8BDC              ; Force speaker low without changing D0..D3.
  70++8BDC              p127_speaker_stop:
  71++8BDC 3A 0F 8C             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  72++8BDF E6 EF                and     0EFh
  73++8BE1 32 0F 8C             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  74++8BE4 D3 FE                out     (P127_SPEAKER_PORT),a
  75++8BE6 C9                   ret
  76++8BE7
  77++8BE7              p127_speaker_get:
  78++8BE7 3A 0F 8C             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  79++8BEA C9                   ret
  80++8BEB
  81++8BEB
  82++8BEB              ; Audible hardware smoke test. Approximately 0.57 s at 7 MHz or 1.14 s at
  83++8BEB              ; 3.5 MHz; pitch therefore depends on the active ATM CPU clock. This is
  84++8BEB              ; intentionally a one-shot diagnostic, not the asynchronous sound service.
  85++8BEB              ; A=low #FE nibble to preserve (caller must supply the live border/tape state).
  86++8BEB              p127_speaker_test_tone:
  87++8BEB F5                   push    af
  88++8BEC C5                   push    bc
  89++8BED D5                   push    de
  90++8BEE E5                   push    hl
  91++8BEF E6 0F                and     P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK
  92++8BF1 CD B1 8B             call    p127_speaker_init
  93++8BF4 11 58 02             ld      de,0258h
  94++8BF7              p127_speaker_test_loop:
  95++8BF7 CD D1 8B             call    p127_speaker_toggle
  96++8BFA 06 FF                ld      b,0FFh
  97++8BFC              p127_speaker_test_delay1:
  98++8BFC 10 FE                djnz    p127_speaker_test_delay1
  99++8BFE 06 FF                ld      b,0FFh
 100++8C00              p127_speaker_test_delay2:
 101++8C00 10 FE                djnz    p127_speaker_test_delay2
 102++8C02 1B                   dec     de
 103++8C03 7A                   ld      a,d
 104++8C04 B3                   or      e
 105++8C05 20 F0                jr      nz,p127_speaker_test_loop
 106++8C07 CD DC 8B             call    p127_speaker_stop
 107++8C0A E1                   pop     hl
 108++8C0B D1                   pop     de
 109++8C0C C1                   pop     bc
 110++8C0D F1                   pop     af
 111++8C0E C9                   ret
 112++8C0F
 113++8C0F 00           p127_speaker_shadow: db 0

   2++8EE6              ; Project127 FAT16 root-directory reader

   6++8EE6              ; Read-only: no writes, DMA, IRQ, IM/EI or paging OUT.

   1++9204              ; ============================================================================
   2++9204              ; Project127 — interrupt safety contract / preflight
   3++9204              ; Module version: 0.6.0
   4++9204              ; Masked IM2 installer with the legacy host-frame ISR chain.
   5++9204              ; Normal boot remains DI-only; live activation is still opt-in.
   6++9204              ; ============================================================================
   7++9204              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   8++9204              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MINOR EQU 6
   9++9204              P127_IRQ_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  10++9204              P127_IRQ_MASKED EQU 1
  11++9204              P127_IRQ_VECTOR_NONE EQU 0
  12++9204              P127_IRQ_MODE_UNSET EQU 0
  13++9204              P127_IRQ_HOST_VECTOR EQU 0038h
  14++9204
  15++9204              ; Boot owns the CPU in a DI-protected phase. A stock Z80 cannot read IM0/1/2
  16++9204              ; directly; LD A,I exposes IFF2 in P/V but is not a general IFF restore path.
  17++9204              ; Therefore this module never guesses prior interrupt state and never EI/IM.
  18++9204              ; The boot installer may establish the vector, mapping and handler ownership
  19++9204              ; tuple while DI remains asserted. Interrupts stay disabled until a separate
  20++9204              ; controlled source/acknowledge test validates live delivery and RETI.
  21++9204              P127_IRQ_READY_VECTOR EQU 1
  22++9204              P127_IRQ_READY_MAP EQU 2
  23++9204              P127_IRQ_READY_HANDLER EQU 4
  24++9204              P127_IRQ_READY_ALL EQU 7
  25++9204

 375++9440              ; Stage 2.15/2.19: resident interrupt frame protocol.
 376++9440              ; Entry contract: CPU has pushed the interrupted PC; W2/P2 code and stack

  38+ A011              p126_atm_video_init:
  38+ A011
  39+ A011 F5                   push    af
  40+ A012 C5                   push    bc
  41+ A013 D5                   push    de
  42+ A014 E5                   push    hl
  43+ A015
  44+ A015                      ; Reset the ATM paging/control latch BEFORE enabling permanent
  45+ A015                      ; DOSEN. On this board #EFF7 is part of the ATM paging control path;
  46+ A015                      ; resetting it after the #0177 shadow-port setup can undo the state
  47+ A015                      ; needed by the TR-DOS overlay.
  48+ A015 AF                   xor     a
  49+ A016 01 F7 EF             ld      bc, #EFF7
  50+ A019 ED 79                out     (c), a
  51+ A01B
  52+ A01B                      ; ROM2=1 (D4) is required for TR-DOS overlay; D3 selects alternate
  53+ A01B                      ; video pages 7/3. This is #18, matching the host display setup.
  54+ A01B 3E 18                ld      a, #18
  55+ A01D 01 FD 7F             ld      bc, #7FFD
  56+ A020 ED 79                out     (c), a
  57+ A022 32 3B 5C             ld      (#5C3B), a
  58+ A025
  59+ A025                      ; Open shadow ports and hold DOSEN permanently (A9=0) using the
  60+ A025                      ; documented TR-DOS ROM trampoline. Do this AFTER the paging reset.
  61+ A025 01 35 A0             ld      bc, p126_atm_ports_open_done
  62+ A028 C5                   push    bc
  63+ A029 01 53 2A             ld      bc, #2A53
  64+ A02C C5                   push    bc
  65+ A02D 01 77 01             ld      bc, #0177
  66+ A030 3E C3                ld      a, #C3
  67+ A032 C3 2F 3D             jp      #3D2F
  68+ A035
  69+ A035              p126_atm_ports_open_done:
  70+ A035                      ; Window 0 must use ROM with D7=1 so its ROM page selection is
  71+ A035                      ; coupled to ROM2/DOSEN. #BF selects the low ROM page with the
  72+ A035                      ; DOSEN-controlled low ROM select: BASIC-48 when DOSEN=0, TR-DOS
  73+ A035                      ; when DOSEN=1. This is the ATM2+ documented auto-TR-DOS mapping.
  74+ A035 3E BF                ld      a, #BF
  75+ A037 01 F7 3F             ld      bc, #3FF7
  76+ A03A ED 79                out     (c), a
  77+ A03C
  78+ A03C
  79+ A03C                      ; Map the alternate screen pages while the 32-byte mode trampoline
  80+ A03C                      ; is installed at #FFEC. Window 3 uses a fixed RAM page here; the
  81+ A03C                      ; TR-DOS auto-select mux is configured separately in window 0.
  82+ A03C 3E 78                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE7
  83+ A03E 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
  84+ A041 ED 79                out     (c), a
  85+ A043 3E 7C                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE3
  86+ A045 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
  87+ A048 ED 79                out     (c), a
  88+ A04A
  89+ A04A                      ; LD BC,#0177 / OUT (C),A / JP p126_atm_video_mode_ready
  90+ A04A 21 EC FF             ld      hl, #FFEC
  91+ A04D 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  92+ A04F 23                   inc     hl
  93+ A050 36 77                ld      (hl), #77
  94+ A052 23                   inc     hl
  95+ A053 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  96+ A055 23                   inc     hl
  97+ A056 36 ED                ld      (hl), #ED
  98+ A058 23                   inc     hl
  99+ A059 36 79                ld      (hl), #79
 100+ A05B 23                   inc     hl
 101+ A05C 36 C3                ld      (hl), #C3
 102+ A05E 23                   inc     hl
 103+ A05F 36 6E                ld      (hl), p126_atm_video_mode_ready & #FF
 104+ A061 23                   inc     hl
 105+ A062 36 A0                ld      (hl), p126_atm_video_mode_ready >> 8
 106+ A064
 107+ A064                      ; ATM Turbo 2+ RG=6: hardware text 80x25.
 108+ A064 3E 06                ld      a,P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25
 109+ A066 32 00 A0             ld      (p126_atm_video_mode),a
 110+ A069 3E C6                ld      a, #C6
 111+ A06B C3 EC FF             jp      #FFEC
 112+ A06E
 113+ A06E              p126_atm_video_mode_ready:
 114+ A06E                      ; Restore CPU-visible host/staging pages. Keep D7 clear on #FFF7 so
 115+ A06E                      ; window 3 remains fixed to RAM page 0 for the TR-DOS staging buffer.
 116+ A06E 3E 7A                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE5
 117+ A070 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
 118+ A073 ED 79                out     (c), a
 119+ A075 3E 7F                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE0
 120+ A077 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
 121+ A07A ED 79                out     (c), a
 122+ A07C
 123+ A07C E1                   pop     hl
 124+ A07D D1                   pop     de
 125+ A07E C1                   pop     bc
 126+ A07F F1                   pop     af
 127+ A080 C9                   ret

 129+ A081
 130+ A081              ; Reopen the permanent ATM shadow-port/DOSEN state after DS12887 access.
 131+ A081              ; #EFF7 bit 7 is the RTC gate; closing it also closes the permanent DOSEN
 132+ A081              ; path. Re-enter the TR-DOS #0177 service and restore RG6 text mode exactly
 133+ A081              ; as in Project126's atm_host implementation.
 134+ A081              p126_atm_restore_dosen:

 598+ A40C
 599+ A40C
 600+ A40C              ; ============================================================================
 601+ A40C              ; Project127 ATM video mode smoke test.
 602+ A40C              ; Reference mode bytes are taken from Project112's tested mode table:
 603+ A40C              ;   RG3 #C3 Sinclair, RG2 #C2 ATM640, RG0 #C0 EGA, RG6 #C6 text.
 604+ A40C              ; W1/W3 are remapped only while the UI owns W3. The trampoline is written
 605+ A40C              ; into the currently mapped video page 3 at #FFEC, then host pages are
 606+ A40C              ; restored immediately after the mode-register write.
 607+ A40C              ; ============================================================================
 608+ A40C              P127_VIDEO_MODE_SINCLAIR EQU #C3
 609+ A40C              P127_VIDEO_MODE_ATM640   EQU #C2
 610+ A40C              P127_VIDEO_MODE_EGA      EQU #C0
 611+ A40C              P127_VIDEO_MODE_TEXT     EQU #C6
 612+ A40C
 613+ A40C              p127_video_mode_selftest:
 614+ A40C                      ; Preserve representative character and attribute bytes from both
 615+ A40C                      ; parity planes. The mode changes themselves must not alter VRAM.
 616+ A40C AF                   xor     a
 617+ A40D 32 C2 A5             ld      (p127_video_capture_phase),a
 618+ A410 CD F0 A4             call    p127_video_capture_signature
 619+ A413 D8                   ret     c
 620+ A414
 621+ A414 3E C3                ld      a,P127_VIDEO_MODE_SINCLAIR
 622+ A416 CD 76 A4             call    p127_video_switch_mode
 623+ A419 D8                   ret     c
 624+ A41A CD AE A5             call    p127_video_visible_delay
 625+ A41D
 626+ A41D 3E C2                ld      a,P127_VIDEO_MODE_ATM640
 627+ A41F CD 76 A4             call    p127_video_switch_mode
 628+ A422 D8                   ret     c
 629+ A423 CD AE A5             call    p127_video_visible_delay
 630+ A426
 631+ A426 3E C0                ld      a,P127_VIDEO_MODE_EGA
 632+ A428 CD 76 A4             call    p127_video_switch_mode
 633+ A42B D8                   ret     c
 634+ A42C CD AE A5             call    p127_video_visible_delay
 635+ A42F
 636+ A42F                      ; Always return the controller to the Project127 UI mode.
 637+ A42F 3E C6                ld      a,P127_VIDEO_MODE_TEXT
 638+ A431 CD 76 A4             call    p127_video_switch_mode
 639+ A434 D8                   ret     c
 640+ A435
 641+ A435 3E 01                ld      a,1
 642+ A437 32 C2 A5             ld      (p127_video_capture_phase),a
 643+ A43A CD F0 A4             call    p127_video_capture_signature

 679+ A476              ; IN A=complete ATM mode-register byte. Carry means W3 UI lease refused.

 796+ A533                      ; This is read-only: no VRAM byte is overwritten by the test.

 829+ A566 C9                   ret

 831+ A567              ; Calculate CRC-16/CCITT (poly #1021, init #FFFF) over both mapped
 832+ A567              ; 16 KiB pages. W1=#4000..#7FFF is P7; W3=#C000..#FFFF is P3.
 833+ A567              ; OUT: p127_video_crc_p7 and p127_video_crc_p3, low byte first.
 834+ A567              p127_video_crc16_pages:
 835+ A567 C5                   push    bc
 836+ A568 D5                   push    de
 837+ A569 E5                   push    hl
 838+ A56A 21 00 40             ld      hl,#4000
 839+ A56D CD 82 A5             call    p127_video_crc16_page
 840+ A570 ED 53 D4 A5          ld      (p127_video_crc_p7),de
 841+ A574 21 00 C0             ld      hl,#C000
 842+ A577 CD 82 A5             call    p127_video_crc16_page
 843+ A57A ED 53 D6 A5          ld      (p127_video_crc_p3),de
 844+ A57E E1                   pop     hl
 845+ A57F D1                   pop     de
 846+ A580 C1                   pop     bc
 847+ A581 C9                   ret
 848+ A582
 849+ A582              ; IN HL=window base. OUT DE=CRC-16/CCITT.
 850+ A582              p127_video_crc16_page:
 851+ A582 11 FF FF             ld      de,#FFFF
 852+ A585 01 00 40             ld      bc,#4000
 853+ A588              p127_video_crc16_byte:
 854+ A588 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 855+ A589 AA                   xor     d
 856+ A58A 57                   ld      d,a
 857+ A58B 3E 08                ld      a,8
 858+ A58D 32 D3 A5             ld      (p127_video_crc_bits),a
 859+ A590              p127_video_crc16_bit:
 860+ A590 CB 23                sla     e
 861+ A592 CB 12                rl      d
 862+ A594 30 08                jr      nc,p127_video_crc16_no_poly
 863+ A596 7A                   ld      a,d
 864+ A597 EE 10                xor     #10
 865+ A599 57                   ld      d,a
 866+ A59A 7B                   ld      a,e
 867+ A59B EE 21                xor     #21
 868+ A59D 5F                   ld      e,a
 869+ A59E              p127_video_crc16_no_poly:
 870+ A59E 3A D3 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_crc_bits)
 871+ A5A1 3D                   dec     a
 872+ A5A2 32 D3 A5             ld      (p127_video_crc_bits),a
 873+ A5A5 20 E9                jr      nz,p127_video_crc16_bit
 874+ A5A7 23                   inc     hl
 875+ A5A8 0B                   dec     bc
 876+ A5A9 78                   ld      a,b
 877+ A5AA B1                   or      c
 878+ A5AB 20 DB                jr      nz,p127_video_crc16_byte
 879+ A5AD C9                   ret

   1+ A5E0              ; ============================================================================
   2+ A5E0              ; Project127 physical ATM RAM page test — Stage 2.23
   3+ A5E0              ; Module version: 0.4.3
   4+ A5E0              ; Runs from W2 (#A000..), with stack in W2. W1 resident/kernel remains fixed.
   5+ A5E0              ; W3 is exclusively leased from the UI and restored to physical page 0.
   6+ A5E0              ; This is the first general-purpose physical W3 remap test.
   7+ A5E0              ; ============================================================================
   8+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   9+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  10+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  11+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE     EQU 04000h
  12+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE   EQU 0C000h
  13+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_PORT     EQU 0FFF7h
  14+ A5E0
  15+ A5E0              ; OUT: carry=1 on any failure. Starts with two pages, then allocates and
  16+ A5E0              ; scans every remaining free physical page under one W3 ownership lease.
  17+ A5E0              ; The probe byte is complemented so it can never equal the saved P0 byte.
  18+ A5E0              ; If the dispatcher is not actually switching, only that one P0 byte is
  19+ A5E0              ; touched and it is restored before returning. Full-page writes start only
  20+ A5E0              ; after the physical switch has been proven.
  21+ A5E0              p127_physmem_batch_selftest:
  22+ A5E0 3E 53                ld      a,'S'
  23+ A5E2 CD 8E AC             call    p127_physmem_trace

  30+ A5F5 32 56 AD             ld      (p127_physmem_saved_valid),a

  64+ A63D 3E 01                ld      a,1
  65+ A63F 32 54 AD             ld      (p127_physmem_acquired),a
  66+ A642
  67+ A642                      ; Preserve one byte from P0 for a non-destructive port-open probe.
  68+ A642 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
  69+ A645 32 5A AD             ld      (p127_physmem_saved_p0),a
  70+ A648 2F                   cpl
  71+ A649 32 5B AD             ld      (p127_physmem_probe),a
  72+ A64C 3E 01                ld      a,1
  73+ A64E 32 56 AD             ld      (p127_physmem_saved_valid),a
  74+ A651
  75+ A651                      ; Select allocated page A and write one complemented probe byte.
  76+ A651 3A 57 AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_page_a)
  77+ A654 CD 14 AD             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
  78+ A657 DA A2 A8             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_map
  79+ A65A 3E 01                ld      a,1
  80+ A65C 32 55 AD             ld      (p127_physmem_mapped),a

  85+ A665 AF                   xor     a

 224+ A7CC                      ; Stage 2.19: exhaust the allocator into a private page-ID list.

 293+ A847                      ; Reverse sweep verifies every page after all other pages have
 294+ A847                      ; already been written. This catches aliases and lost page contents.

 420+ A924              p127_physmem_cleanup_done:
 421+ A924                      ; Reallocation must return the same lowest free page and release it.

 442+ A951                      ; The live page sweep ran with W3 exclusively leased, so progress was
 443+ A951                      ; mirrored to #EF only. Replay the compact page list now that W3=P0
 444+ A951                      ; and the UI owner has been restored.

 447+ A955 C9                   ret
 448+ A956              p127_physmem_cleanup_return_error:
 449+ A956 37                   scf
 450+ A957 C9                   ret
 451+ A958
 452+ A958              ; Replay all successful physical-page selections on the visible ATM console.
 453+ A958              ; The 57 IDs are compacted into two wrapped lines rather than 114 long lines.
 454+ A958              p127_physmem_replay_page_log:
 455+ A958                      ; The W3 sweep and its cleanup may leave the video-bank selector in a
 456+ A958                      ; different state even though the CPU windows have been restored.
 457+ A958                      ; Reapply the proven Project126/ATM RG6 setup only after the W3 lease
 458+ A958                      ; is released and UI ownership is active. This preserves screen RAM.
 459+ A958 CD 11 A0             call    p126_atm_video_init
 460+ A95B
 461+ A95B                      ; Fixed-row probe: if this label is visible, the selected alternate
 462+ A95B                      ; text bank (P7 characters / P3 attributes) and PrintAt path agree.

 482+ A985 20 ED                jr      nz,p127_physmem_replay_fill_loop

 487+ A991

 512+ A9C4                      ; Put the decisive result in the fixed status panel as well as the
 513+ A9C4                      ; scrolling log. PrintAt addresses rows directly, so this does not
 514+ A9C4                      ; depend on the current scrolling cursor after the long page replay.

 518+ A9CB CD 63 A1             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintAt
 519+ A9CE 21 4A AA             ld      hl,p127_physmem_screen_status_line1
 520+ A9D1 3E 03                ld      a,3
 521+ A9D3 0E 01                ld      c,1
 522+ A9D5 CD 63 A1             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintAt
 523+ A9D8 C9                   ret

 525+ A9D9              ; HL=zero-terminated string. The console routine preserves HL.
 526+ A9D9              p127_physmem_screen_puts:
 527+ A9D9 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 528+ A9DA B7                   or      a
 529+ A9DB C8                   ret     z
 530+ A9DC 23                   inc     hl
 531+ A9DD E5                   push    hl
 532+ A9DE CD D8 A1             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintChar

 570+ AA8D              p127_physmem_cleanup_error:
 571+ AA8D 3E FF                ld      a,0FFh
 572+ AA8F 32 51 AD             ld      (p127_physmem_error),a
 573+ AA92 37                   scf
 574+ AA93 C9                   ret
 575+ AA94
 576+ AA94              ; Check the seven fixed reservation states: P0/P1/P2/P3/P4/P5/P7.
 577+ AA94              ; OUT carry=1 if any protected page is not RESERVED.
 578+ AA94              p127_physmem_check_reserved:
 579+ AA94 21 AC 75             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 580+ AA97 06 08                ld      b,8
 581+ AA99 0E 00                ld      c,0
 582+ AA9B              p127_physmem_reserved_loop:
 583+ AA9B 79                   ld      a,c
 584+ AA9C FE 06                cp      6
 585+ AA9E 28 2E                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_skip
 586+ AAA0 FE 07                cp      7
 587+ AAA2 28 1A                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 588+ AAA4 FE 00                cp      0
 589+ AAA6 28 16                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 590+ AAA8 FE 01                cp      1
 591+ AAAA 28 12                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 592+ AAAC FE 02                cp      2
 593+ AAAE 28 0E                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 594+ AAB0 FE 03                cp      3
 595+ AAB2 28 0A                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 596+ AAB4 FE 04                cp      4
 597+ AAB6 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 598+ AAB8 FE 05                cp      5
 599+ AABA 28 02                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 600+ AABC 18 10                jr      p127_physmem_reserved_skip
 601+ AABE              p127_physmem_reserved_check:
 602+ AABE C5                   push    bc
 603+ AABF E5                   push    hl
 604+ AAC0 59                   ld      e,c
 605+ AAC1 16 00                ld      d,0
 606+ AAC3 21 AC 75             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 607+ AAC6 19                   add     hl,de
 608+ AAC7 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 609+ AAC8 FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
 610+ AACA E1                   pop     hl
 611+ AACB C1                   pop     bc
 612+ AACC 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_reserved_fail
 613+ AACE              p127_physmem_reserved_skip:
 614+ AACE 0C                   inc     c
 615+ AACF 10 CA                djnz    p127_physmem_reserved_loop
 616+ AAD1 B7                   or      a
 617+ AAD2 C9                   ret
 618+ AAD3              p127_physmem_reserved_fail:
 619+ AAD3 37                   scf
 620+ AAD4 C9                   ret
 621+ AAD5
 622+ AAD5              ; Every page in the private list must remain ALLOCATED during the sweep.
 623+ AAD5              p127_physmem_check_allocated:
 624+ AAD5 3A 52 AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 625+ AAD8 47                   ld      b,a
 626+ AAD9 21 5F AD             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list
 627+ AADC              p127_physmem_alloc_state_loop:
 628+ AADC 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 629+ AADD 5F                   ld      e,a
 630+ AADE 16 00                ld      d,0
 631+ AAE0 C5                   push    bc
 632+ AAE1 E5                   push    hl
 633+ AAE2 21 AC 75             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 634+ AAE5 19                   add     hl,de
 635+ AAE6 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 636+ AAE7 FE 02                cp      P127_MEM_ALLOCATED
 637+ AAE9 E1                   pop     hl
 638+ AAEA C1                   pop     bc
 639+ AAEB 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_alloc_state_fail
 640+ AAED 23                   inc     hl
 641+ AAEE 10 EC                djnz    p127_physmem_alloc_state_loop
 642+ AAF0 B7                   or      a
 643+ AAF1 C9                   ret
 644+ AAF2              p127_physmem_alloc_state_fail:
 645+ AAF2 37                   scf
 646+ AAF3 C9                   ret
 647+ AAF4
 648+ AAF4              ; After release, every non-reserved page must be FREE; reserved pages
 649+ AAF4              ; are checked separately by p127_physmem_check_reserved.
 650+ AAF4              p127_physmem_check_free_state:
 651+ AAF4 21 AC 75             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 652+ AAF7 06 40                ld      b,64
 653+ AAF9 0E 00                ld      c,0
 654+ AAFB              p127_physmem_free_state_loop:
 655+ AAFB 79                   ld      a,c
 656+ AAFC FE 00                cp      0
 657+ AAFE 28 1E                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 658+ AB00 FE 01                cp      1
 659+ AB02 28 1A                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 660+ AB04 FE 02                cp      2
 661+ AB06 28 16                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 662+ AB08 FE 03                cp      3
 663+ AB0A 28 12                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 664+ AB0C FE 04                cp      4
 665+ AB0E 28 0E                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 666+ AB10 FE 05                cp      5
 667+ AB12 28 0A                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 668+ AB14 FE 07                cp      7
 669+ AB16 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 670+ AB18 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 671+ AB19 B7                   or      a
 672+ AB1A 20 0D                jr      nz,p127_physmem_free_state_fail
 673+ AB1C 18 05                jr      p127_physmem_free_state_next
 674+ AB1E              p127_physmem_free_state_reserved:
 675+ AB1E 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 676+ AB1F FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
 677+ AB21 20 06                jr      nz,p127_physmem_free_state_fail
 678+ AB23              p127_physmem_free_state_next:
 679+ AB23 23                   inc     hl
 680+ AB24 0C                   inc     c
 681+ AB25 10 D4                djnz    p127_physmem_free_state_loop
 682+ AB27 B7                   or      a
 683+ AB28 C9                   ret
 684+ AB29              p127_physmem_free_state_fail:
 685+ AB29 37                   scf
 686+ AB2A C9                   ret
 687+ AB2B
 688+ AB2B              ; Emit one printable phase marker directly on the established #EF debug port.
 689+ AB2B              ; This remains available while the UI is quiesced and W3 is leased.
 690+ AB2B
 691+ AB2B              ; ============================================================================
 692+ AB2B              ; Stage 2.20 — guarded physical-page API.
 693+ AB2B              ; All code and stack remain in W2. The only hardware mapping operation is W3.
 694+ AB2B              ; Public ABI:
 695+ AB2B              ;   alloc_page: OUT A=allocated page, carry=1 if exhausted.
 696+ AB2B              ;   free_page:  IN A=allocated page, carry=1 if invalid or lease active.
 697+ AB2B              ;   w3_begin:  quiesce UI, acquire W3, save P0 byte; DI remains in force.
 698+ AB2B              ;   w3_map:    IN A=allocated page; valid only inside an active W3 lease.
 699+ AB2B              ;   w3_end:    restore W3=P0 and saved byte, release lease, resume UI.
 700+ AB2B              ; No API maps W0/W1/W2, frees a currently mapped page, or enables IRQs.
 701+ AB2B              ; ============================================================================
 702+ AB2B              p127_physmem_alloc_page:
 703+ AB2B C3 4C 73             jp      p127_mem_alloc
 704+ AB2E
 705+ AB2E              p127_physmem_free_page:
 706+ AB2E 32 8B AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_page_arg),a
 707+ AB31 3A 86 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 708+ AB34 B7                   or      a
 709+ AB35 C2 E8 AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 710+ AB38 3A 8B AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 711+ AB3B 47                   ld      b,a
 712+ AB3C 3A A0 84             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 713+ AB3F B8                   cp      b
 714+ AB40 CA E8 AB             jp      z,p127_physmem_api_reject
 715+ AB43 3A 8B AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 716+ AB46 C3 62 73             jp      p127_mem_free
 717+ AB49
 718+ AB49              ; Begin a bounded W3 lease. Bootstrap mapping must be W3=P0.
 719+ AB49              p127_physmem_w3_begin:
 720+ AB49 F3                   di
 721+ AB4A 3A 86 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 722+ AB4D B7                   or      a
 723+ AB4E C2 E8 AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 724+ AB51 3A A0 84             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 725+ AB54 B7                   or      a
 726+ AB55 C2 E8 AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 727+ AB58 CD F5 70             call    p127_w3_quiesce
 728+ AB5B D8                   ret     c
 729+ AB5C 3E 01                ld      a,1
 730+ AB5E 32 87 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 731+ AB61 CD 14 71             call    p127_w3_handoff_acquire
 732+ AB64 38 15                jr      c,p127_physmem_w3_begin_rollback
 733+ AB66 3E 01                ld      a,1
 734+ AB68 32 88 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 735+ AB6B 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 736+ AB6E 32 8A AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_p0),a
 737+ AB71 3E 01                ld      a,1
 738+ AB73 32 89 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 739+ AB76 32 86 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 740+ AB79 B7                   or      a
 741+ AB7A C9                   ret
 742+ AB7B              p127_physmem_w3_begin_rollback:
 743+ AB7B CD 3C 71             call    p127_w3_resume
 744+ AB7E AF                   xor     a
 745+ AB7F 32 87 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 746+ AB82 37                   scf
 747+ AB83 C9                   ret
 748+ AB84
 749+ AB84              ; Map only an allocator-owned page into W3.
 750+ AB84              p127_physmem_w3_map:
 751+ AB84 32 8B AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_page_arg),a
 752+ AB87 3A 86 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 753+ AB8A FE 01                cp      1
 754+ AB8C C2 E8 AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 755+ AB8F 3A 8B AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 756+ AB92 FE 40                cp      P127_PHYSMEM_MAX_PAGES
 757+ AB94 D2 E8 AB             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_reject
 758+ AB97 5F                   ld      e,a
 759+ AB98 16 00                ld      d,0
 760+ AB9A 21 AC 75             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 761+ AB9D 19                   add     hl,de
 762+ AB9E 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 763+ AB9F FE 02                cp      P127_MEM_ALLOCATED
 764+ ABA1 C2 E8 AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 765+ ABA4 3A 8B AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 766+ ABA7 C3 14 AD             jp      p127_physmem_set_w3
 767+ ABAA
 768+ ABAA              ; End the lease in recovery order. If release/resume fails, state flags are
 769+ ABAA              ; retained so the caller can retry this routine without losing ownership.
 770+ ABAA              p127_physmem_w3_end:
 771+ ABAA 3A 86 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 772+ ABAD FE 01                cp      1
 773+ ABAF C2 E8 AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 774+ ABB2 AF                   xor     a
 775+ ABB3 CD 14 AD             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
 776+ ABB6 D8                   ret     c
 777+ ABB7 3A 89 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_saved_valid)
 778+ ABBA B7                   or      a
 779+ ABBB 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_release
 780+ ABBD 3A 8A AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_saved_p0)
 781+ ABC0 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
 782+ ABC3              p127_physmem_w3_end_release:
 783+ ABC3 3A 88 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_acquired)
 784+ ABC6 B7                   or      a
 785+ ABC7 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_resume
 786+ ABC9 CD 28 71             call    p127_w3_handoff_release
 787+ ABCC D8                   ret     c
 788+ ABCD AF                   xor     a
 789+ ABCE 32 88 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 790+ ABD1              p127_physmem_w3_end_resume:
 791+ ABD1 3A 87 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_quiesced)
 792+ ABD4 B7                   or      a
 793+ ABD5 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_done
 794+ ABD7 CD 3C 71             call    p127_w3_resume
 795+ ABDA D8                   ret     c
 796+ ABDB AF                   xor     a
 797+ ABDC 32 87 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 798+ ABDF              p127_physmem_w3_end_done:
 799+ ABDF AF                   xor     a
 800+ ABE0 32 89 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 801+ ABE3 32 86 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 802+ ABE6 B7                   or      a
 803+ ABE7 C9                   ret
 804+ ABE8
 805+ ABE8              p127_physmem_api_reject:
 806+ ABE8 37                   scf
 807+ ABE9 C9                   ret
 808+ ABEA
 809+ ABEA              ; Runtime API contract test. It proves rejection paths, allocated-page-only
 810+ ABEA              ; mapping, mapped-page free protection, P0 byte restoration and UI recovery.
 811+ ABEA              p127_physmem_api_selftest:
 812+ ABEA AF                   xor     a
 813+ ABEB 32 86 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 814+ ABEE 32 87 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 815+ ABF1 32 88 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 816+ ABF4 32 89 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 817+ ABF7 3E 01                ld      a,1
 818+ ABF9 CD 2E AB             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 819+ ABFC D2 84 AC             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 820+ ABFF CD 2B AB             call    p127_physmem_alloc_page
 821+ AC02 DA 84 AC             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 822+ AC05 32 8C AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_test_page),a
 823+ AC08 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 824+ AC0B 32 8D AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_test_p0),a
 825+ AC0E CD 49 AB             call    p127_physmem_w3_begin
 826+ AC11 DA 7E AC             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_free
 827+ AC14                      ; A reserved page must never be accepted by the map API.
 828+ AC14 3E 01                ld      a,1
 829+ AC16 CD 84 AB             call    p127_physmem_w3_map
 830+ AC19 D2 7B AC             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 831+ AC1C 3A 8C AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 832+ AC1F CD 84 AB             call    p127_physmem_w3_map
 833+ AC22 DA 7B AC             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 834+ AC25                      ; The currently mapped page cannot be freed while the lease is held.
 835+ AC25 3A 8C AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 836+ AC28 CD 2E AB             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 837+ AC2B D2 7B AC             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 838+ AC2E 3E A5                ld      a,0A5h
 839+ AC30 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
 840+ AC33 CD AA AB             call    p127_physmem_w3_end
 841+ AC36 DA 84 AC             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 842+ AC39 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 843+ AC3C 47                   ld      b,a
 844+ AC3D 3A 8D AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_p0)
 845+ AC40 B8                   cp      b
 846+ AC41 C2 84 AC             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 847+ AC44 3A 8C AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 848+ AC47 CD 2E AB             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 849+ AC4A DA 84 AC             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 850+ AC4D                      ; First-fit must return the same page after release.
 851+ AC4D CD 2B AB             call    p127_physmem_alloc_page
 852+ AC50 DA 84 AC             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 853+ AC53 47                   ld      b,a
 854+ AC54 3A 8C AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 855+ AC57 B8                   cp      b
 856+ AC58 C2 84 AC             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 857+ AC5B 78                   ld      a,b
 858+ AC5C CD 2E AB             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 859+ AC5F DA 84 AC             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 860+ AC62 3A 62 72             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 861+ AC65 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 862+ AC67 C2 84 AC             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 863+ AC6A 3A 63 72             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 864+ AC6D FE 01                cp      P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 865+ AC6F C2 84 AC             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 866+ AC72 3A A0 84             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 867+ AC75 B7                   or      a
 868+ AC76 C2 84 AC             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 869+ AC79 B7                   or      a
 870+ AC7A C9                   ret
 871+ AC7B
 872+ AC7B              p127_physmem_api_test_end:
 873+ AC7B CD AA AB             call    p127_physmem_w3_end
 874+ AC7E              p127_physmem_api_test_free:
 875+ AC7E 3A 8C AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 876+ AC81 CD 2E AB             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 877+ AC84              p127_physmem_api_test_fail:
 878+ AC84 37                   scf
 879+ AC85 C9                   ret
 880+ AC86
 881+ AC86 00           p127_physmem_api_active: db 0
 882+ AC87 00           p127_physmem_api_quiesced: db 0
 883+ AC88 00           p127_physmem_api_acquired: db 0
 884+ AC89 00           p127_physmem_api_saved_valid: db 0
 885+ AC8A 00           p127_physmem_api_saved_p0: db 0
 886+ AC8B 00           p127_physmem_api_page_arg: db 0
 887+ AC8C 00           p127_physmem_api_test_page: db 0
 888+ AC8D 00           p127_physmem_api_test_p0: db 0
 889+ AC8E
 890+ AC8E              p127_physmem_trace:
 891+ AC8E 01 EF 00             ld      bc,00EFh
 892+ AC91 ED 79                out     (c),a
 893+ AC93 C9                   ret
 894+ AC94
 895+ AC94              ; Emit progress for each physical page while the UI is quiesced.
 896+ AC94              ; A='F' prints FILL PAGE ID=xx; A='V' prints VERIFY PAGE ID=xx.
 897+ AC94              ; Preserve every register because the caller owns the page-list cursor.
 898+ AC94              p127_physmem_trace_page:
 899+ AC94 F5                   push    af
 900+ AC95 C5                   push    bc
 901+ AC96 D5                   push    de
 902+ AC97 E5                   push    hl
 903+ AC98 FE 46                cp      'F'
 904+ AC9A 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_trace_page_verify
 905+ AC9C 21 E6 AC             ld      hl,p127_physmem_trace_fill_text
 906+ AC9F 18 03                jr      p127_physmem_trace_page_text
 907+ ACA1              p127_physmem_trace_page_verify:
 908+ ACA1 21 F4 AC             ld      hl,p127_physmem_trace_verify_text
 909+ ACA4              p127_physmem_trace_page_text:
 910+ ACA4 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 911+ ACA5 B7                   or      a

 924+ ACBD CD E0 AC             call    p127_physmem_trace_char

 926+ ACC1 D1                   pop     de
 927+ ACC2 C1                   pop     bc
 928+ ACC3 F1                   pop     af
 929+ ACC4 C9                   ret

 931+ ACC5              p127_physmem_trace_hex8:
 932+ ACC5 F5                   push    af
 933+ ACC6 E6 F0                and     0F0h
 934+ ACC8 0F                   rrca
 935+ ACC9 0F                   rrca
 936+ ACCA 0F                   rrca
 937+ ACCB 0F                   rrca

 939+ ACCF F1                   pop     af

 945+ ACD6 16 00                ld      d,0

 963+ AD14              ; IN A=physical RAM page ID. Program only W3 (#FFF7), never W0/W1/W2.
 964+ AD14              ; D6=1 selects RAM, D7=0 selects the inverse D0-D5 page ID.
 965+ AD14              p127_physmem_set_w3:
 966+ AD14 32 59 AD             ld      (p127_physmem_page_arg),a
 967+ AD17 CD BD 82             call    p127_map_encode_page

 973+ AD26 B7                   or      a
 974+ AD27 C9                   ret
 975+ AD28
 976+ AD28              ; IN D=pattern seed. Byte(address)=ROL(address_low) XOR address_high XOR D.
 977+ AD28              ; The same address produces a different expected value at each seed.
 978+ AD28              p127_physmem_fill_pattern:
 979+ AD28 21 00 C0             ld      hl,P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE
 980+ AD2B 01 00 40             ld      bc,P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE
 981+ AD2E              .p127_physmem_fill_pattern_loop:
 982+ AD2E 7D                   ld      a,l
 983+ AD2F 07                   rlca
 984+ AD30 AC                   xor     h
 985+ AD31 AA                   xor     d
 986+ AD32 77                   ld      (hl),a
 987+ AD33 23                   inc     hl
 988+ AD34 0B                   dec     bc
 989+ AD35 78                   ld      a,b
 990+ AD36 B1                   or      c
 991+ AD37 20 F5                jr      nz,.p127_physmem_fill_pattern_loop
 992+ AD39 C9                   ret
 993+ AD3A
 994+ AD3A              ; IN D=pattern seed. Verify all 16 KiB against the address-derived pattern.
 995+ AD3A              p127_physmem_verify_pattern:
 996+ AD3A 21 00 C0             ld      hl,P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE
 997+ AD3D 01 00 40             ld      bc,P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE
 998+ AD40              .p127_physmem_verify_pattern_loop:
 999+ AD40 7D                   ld      a,l
1000+ AD41 07                   rlca
1001+ AD42 AC                   xor     h
1002+ AD43 AA                   xor     d
1003+ AD44 BE                   cp      (hl)
1004+ AD45 20 08                jr      nz,.p127_physmem_verify_pattern_fail
1005+ AD47 23                   inc     hl
1006+ AD48 0B                   dec     bc
1007+ AD49 78                   ld      a,b
1008+ AD4A B1                   or      c
1009+ AD4B 20 F3                jr      nz,.p127_physmem_verify_pattern_loop
1010+ AD4D B7                   or      a
1011+ AD4E C9                   ret
1012+ AD4F              .p127_physmem_verify_pattern_fail:
1013+ AD4F 37                   scf
1014+ AD50 C9                   ret

  94  AD9F                      INCLUDE "src/kernel/p127_keyboard.asm"
# file opened: p127_keyboard.asm
   1+ AD9F              ; ============================================================================
   2+ AD9F              ; Project127 — ATM XT keyboard interface
   3+ AD9F              ; Version: 0.2.2
   4+ AD9F              ;
   5+ AD9F              ; ATM Turbo 2+ host reads the Unreal Speccy ATM_KBD interface through ULA
   6+ AD9F              ; port #FE. The emulator must have [Input] ATMKBD=1.
   7+ AD9F              ;
   8+ AD9F              ; ATM_KBD protocol:
   9+ AD9F              ;   read B=#55 -> #AA handshake
  10+ AD9F              ;   read B=#08 -> command 8 (select mode)
  11+ AD9F              ;   read B=mode -> #FF acknowledgement
  12+ AD9F              ;   mode 2: B=#00/#40/#80/#C0 reads R2/R3/R4/R5
  13+ AD9F              ;   mode 3: B=#00 reads and clears lastscan
  14+ AD9F              ;
  15+ AD9F              ; No NeoGS, DMA mailbox, external keyboard controller or IRQ is assumed.
  16+ AD9F              ; If ATMKBD is disabled, retain a ZX 8x5 matrix diagnostic fallback only.
  17+ AD9F              ; IM/EI remain disabled.
  18+ AD9F              ; ============================================================================
  19+ AD9F              P127_KBD_PORT              EQU #FE
  20+ AD9F              P127_KBD_MODE_XT           EQU 2
  21+ AD9F              P127_KBD_MODE_EVENT        EQU 3
  22+ AD9F              ; 41 full 16-bit software delays ~= 10 s at 7 MHz.
  23+ AD9F              P127_KBD_IDLE_WINDOWS       EQU 41
  24+ AD9F              P127_KBD_ROW_MASK          EQU #1F
  25+ AD9F
  26+ AD9F              ; Public boot monitor. All reads are performed by the ATM Z80.
  27+ AD9F              p127_keyboard_monitor:
  28+ AD9F AF                   xor     a
  29+ ADA0 32 E0 AF             ld      (p127_kbd_xt_active),a
  30+ ADA3 32 F9 AF             ld      (p127_kbd_snapshot_valid),a
  31+ ADA6 21 68 AF             ld      hl,p127_kbd_msg_init
  32+ ADA9 CD 21 63             call    p127_puts
  33+ ADAC 3E 29                ld      a,P127_KBD_IDLE_WINDOWS
  34+ ADAE 32 DD AF             ld      (p127_kbd_idle_windows),a
  35+ ADB1
  36+ ADB1 3E 02                ld      a,P127_KBD_MODE_XT
  37+ ADB3 CD 02 AE             call    p127_keyboard_set_mode
  38+ ADB6 38 0D                jr      c,p127_keyboard_legacy_init
  39+ ADB8
  40+ ADB8 3E 01                ld      a,1
  41+ ADBA 32 E0 AF             ld      (p127_kbd_xt_active),a
  42+ ADBD 21 75 AF             ld      hl,p127_kbd_msg_xt_ready
  43+ ADC0 CD 21 63             call    p127_puts
  44+ ADC3 18 06                jr      p127_keyboard_monitor_loop
  45+ ADC5
  46+ ADC5              p127_keyboard_legacy_init:
  47+ ADC5 21 8B AF             ld      hl,p127_kbd_msg_legacy
  48+ ADC8 CD 21 63             call    p127_puts
  49+ ADCB
  50+ ADCB              p127_keyboard_monitor_loop:
  51+ ADCB AF                   xor     a
  52+ ADCC 32 DE AF             ld      (p127_kbd_activity),a
  53+ ADCF 3A E0 AF             ld      a,(p127_kbd_xt_active)
  54+ ADD2 B7                   or      a
  55+ ADD3 28 05                jr      z,p127_keyboard_monitor_legacy
  56+ ADD5 CD 48 AE             call    p127_keyboard_poll_xt
  57+ ADD8 18 03                jr      p127_keyboard_monitor_pace
  58+ ADDA              p127_keyboard_monitor_legacy:
  59+ ADDA CD EC AE             call    p127_keyboard_poll_matrix
  60+ ADDD              p127_keyboard_monitor_pace:
  61+ ADDD                      ; Any make/break or modifier/matrix transition restarts the idle window.
  62+ ADDD 3A DE AF             ld      a,(p127_kbd_activity)
  63+ ADE0 B7                   or      a
  64+ ADE1 28 05                jr      z,p127_keyboard_monitor_delay_start
  65+ ADE3 3E 29                ld      a,P127_KBD_IDLE_WINDOWS
  66+ ADE5 32 DD AF             ld      (p127_kbd_idle_windows),a
  67+ ADE8              p127_keyboard_monitor_delay_start:
  68+ ADE8                      ; Bounded software pacing; no PIT or interrupt dependency.
  69+ ADE8 11 FF FF             ld      de,#FFFF
  70+ ADEB              p127_keyboard_monitor_delay:
  71+ ADEB 1B                   dec     de
  72+ ADEC 7A                   ld      a,d
  73+ ADED B3                   or      e
  74+ ADEE 20 FB                jr      nz,p127_keyboard_monitor_delay
  75+ ADF0 3A DD AF             ld      a,(p127_kbd_idle_windows)
  76+ ADF3 3D                   dec     a
  77+ ADF4 32 DD AF             ld      (p127_kbd_idle_windows),a
  78+ ADF7 20 D2                jr      nz,p127_keyboard_monitor_loop
  79+ ADF9 21 D0 AF             ld      hl,p127_kbd_msg_done
  80+ ADFC CD 21 63             call    p127_puts
  81+ ADFF C3 1D 63             jp      p127_idle
  82+ AE02
  83+ AE02              ; Enter ATM_KBD mode A using the exact Unreal Speccy ATM_KBD::read handshake.
  84+ AE02              ; Carry is set if any of the three protocol responses is unexpected.
  85+ AE02              p127_keyboard_set_mode:
  86+ AE02 32 DF AF             ld      (p127_kbd_requested_mode),a
  87+ AE05 01 FE 55             ld      bc,#55FE
  88+ AE08 ED 78                in      a,(c)
  89+ AE0A FE AA                cp      #AA
  90+ AE0C 20 17                jr      nz,p127_keyboard_mode_fail
  91+ AE0E 01 FE 08             ld      bc,#08FE
  92+ AE11 ED 78                in      a,(c)
  93+ AE13 FE 08                cp      8
  94+ AE15 20 0E                jr      nz,p127_keyboard_mode_fail
  95+ AE17 3A DF AF             ld      a,(p127_kbd_requested_mode)
  96+ AE1A 47                   ld      b,a
  97+ AE1B 0E FE                ld      c,P127_KBD_PORT
  98+ AE1D ED 78                in      a,(c)
  99+ AE1F FE FF                cp      #FF
 100+ AE21 20 02                jr      nz,p127_keyboard_mode_fail
 101+ AE23 B7                   or      a
 102+ AE24 C9                   ret
 103+ AE25              p127_keyboard_mode_fail:
 104+ AE25 37                   scf
 105+ AE26 C9                   ret
 106+ AE27
 107+ AE27              ; Read mode 2 registers. R2 is read-to-clear; R3/R4/R5 are state registers.
 108+ AE27              p127_keyboard_read_mode2:
 109+ AE27 01 FE 00             ld      bc,#00FE
 110+ AE2A ED 78                in      a,(c)
 111+ AE2C 32 E2 AF             ld      (p127_kbd_r2),a
 112+ AE2F 01 FE 40             ld      bc,#40FE
 113+ AE32 ED 78                in      a,(c)
 114+ AE34 32 E3 AF             ld      (p127_kbd_r3),a
 115+ AE37 01 FE 80             ld      bc,#80FE
 116+ AE3A ED 78                in      a,(c)
 117+ AE3C 32 E4 AF             ld      (p127_kbd_r4),a
 118+ AE3F 01 FE C0             ld      bc,#C0FE
 119+ AE42 ED 78                in      a,(c)
 120+ AE44 32 E5 AF             ld      (p127_kbd_r5),a
 121+ AE47 C9                   ret
 122+ AE48
 123+ AE48              ; Poll one event plus the current ATM modifier/extended-key registers.
 124+ AE48              ; lastscan bit 7 means key release. R5 carries key-class/extended metadata.
 125+ AE48              p127_keyboard_poll_xt:
 126+ AE48 CD 27 AE             call    p127_keyboard_read_mode2
 127+ AE4B 3E 03                ld      a,P127_KBD_MODE_EVENT
 128+ AE4D CD 02 AE             call    p127_keyboard_set_mode
 129+ AE50 38 4D                jr      c,p127_keyboard_xt_recover
 130+ AE52 01 FE 00             ld      bc,#00FE
 131+ AE55 ED 78                in      a,(c)
 132+ AE57 32 E1 AF             ld      (p127_kbd_lastscan),a
 133+ AE5A
 134+ AE5A 3E 02                ld      a,P127_KBD_MODE_XT
 135+ AE5C CD 02 AE             call    p127_keyboard_set_mode
 136+ AE5F 38 3E                jr      c,p127_keyboard_xt_recover
 137+ AE61
 138+ AE61 3A E1 AF             ld      a,(p127_kbd_lastscan)
 139+ AE64 B7                   or      a
 140+ AE65 20 1D                jr      nz,p127_keyboard_xt_activity
 141+ AE67 3A E3 AF             ld      a,(p127_kbd_r3)
 142+ AE6A 47                   ld      b,a
 143+ AE6B 3A E6 AF             ld      a,(p127_kbd_prev_r3)
 144+ AE6E B8                   cp      b
 145+ AE6F 20 13                jr      nz,p127_keyboard_xt_activity
 146+ AE71 3A E4 AF             ld      a,(p127_kbd_r4)
 147+ AE74 47                   ld      b,a
 148+ AE75 3A E7 AF             ld      a,(p127_kbd_prev_r4)
 149+ AE78 B8                   cp      b
 150+ AE79 20 09                jr      nz,p127_keyboard_xt_activity
 151+ AE7B 3A E5 AF             ld      a,(p127_kbd_r5)
 152+ AE7E 47                   ld      b,a
 153+ AE7F 3A E8 AF             ld      a,(p127_kbd_prev_r5)
 154+ AE82 B8                   cp      b
 155+ AE83 C8                   ret     z
 156+ AE84
 157+ AE84              p127_keyboard_xt_activity:
 158+ AE84 3E 01                ld      a,1
 159+ AE86 32 DE AF             ld      (p127_kbd_activity),a
 160+ AE89              p127_keyboard_xt_log:
 161+ AE89 CD A5 AE             call    p127_keyboard_log_xt
 162+ AE8C 3A E3 AF             ld      a,(p127_kbd_r3)
 163+ AE8F 32 E6 AF             ld      (p127_kbd_prev_r3),a
 164+ AE92 3A E4 AF             ld      a,(p127_kbd_r4)
 165+ AE95 32 E7 AF             ld      (p127_kbd_prev_r4),a
 166+ AE98 3A E5 AF             ld      a,(p127_kbd_r5)
 167+ AE9B 32 E8 AF             ld      (p127_kbd_prev_r5),a
 168+ AE9E C9                   ret
 169+ AE9F
 170+ AE9F              ; If mode switching fails, retry the mode-2 handshake on the next pass.
 171+ AE9F              p127_keyboard_xt_recover:
